                    Point Z

                                                                                            ME32123R0701500020001

                                     Fig.13-1 Fixed Deep Drilling Cycle 2

X, Y : Coordinates of hole position       F  : Feedrate

Z  : Bottom of hole                       FA : Feedrate for QC

R : Point R                               FB : Final feedrate after gradual deceleration

J  : Depth of cut per continuous pecking  FC : Feedrate for QD

Q : Constant speed zone                   S : Spindle speed before gradual deceleration

QC : Initial pecking zone                 SC : Spindle speed after gradual deceleration

QD : Cut-through zone                     P : Dwell time

                                          KA : Runoff per peck

                                          KB : Runoff per series of successive pecks
                                                                    6103-E P-108
          SECTION 13 FIXED DEEP HOLE DRILLING CYCLE 2

          I : Feed per peck

          J : Feed per series of successive pecks

   I      KA: Runoff per cycle

      KA  KB: Runoff per continuous peck

J  I

                                                                                   KB

                                                                                                                                                                                      ME32123R0701500020002

                                        Fig.13-2 A Series of Successive Pecks

Machining Sequence

Procedure:

     1 Positioning along the X and Y directions at a rapid feedrate
     2 Positioning to the point R level at a rapid feedrate
Drilling the initial pecking zone
     3 Drilling the pecking amount QC at the spindle speed S and feedrate FA, by spindle rotation

          in CW direction.
     4 Returning to the point R level at a rapid feedrate (to eject chips)
Machining the constant speed zone
     5 Running off the point machined at a rapid feedrate by the distance equivalent to KB
     6 Drilling only for the depth I at the feedrate F
     7 Running off by the distance of KA at a rapid feedrate.
     8 Drilling only for the depth J by repeating the steps 6 and 7
     9 Returning to the point R level at a rapid feedrate (to eject chips)
    10 Drilling the zone Q by repeating the steps 5 through 9
    11 Returning to the point R level at a rapid feedrate (to eject chips)
Machining the gradual deceleration zone
    12 Running off the machined point by the distance equivalent to KB at a rapid feedrate
    13 Drilling from point Z over zone QD by repeating steps 5 through 9 while decreasing the

          rapid feedrate and the spindle speed (to have a linear relationship among the feedrate,
          spindle speed and machining depth, see "Fig. 13-3")
    14 Returning to the point R level at a rapid feedrate (to eject chips)
Drilling the cut-through zone
    15 Run off the point machined at a rapid feedrate by the distance equivalent to KB.
    16 Drilling up to the point Z at the feedrate FC and spindle speed SC by repeating the steps 5
          through 9
    17 Dwelling at the point Z level for P seconds
    18 Returning to the return point at a rapid feedrate
                                                                            6103-E P-109
                  SECTION 13 FIXED DEEP HOLE DRILLING CYCLE 2

13-3. Supplement

(1) The KA/KB return operation can be changed to G1 using optional parameter bit No. 59, bit 4.
      For the feedrate during the G1 operation, see the value of optional parameter long word No. 14.

(2) When omitting the runoff by KA/KB, see the value of optional parameter long word. The runoff
      distance of KA can be designated to be zero. In this case, there is no step operation in zone I.
          KA: Optional parameter long word No. 1
          KB: Optional parameter long word No. 2

(3) If 0 is assigned for the Q/QC/QD commands, the relevant zone will not be machined. If Q/QC/
      QD is omitted when initially commanded, the system considers that zero has been assigned.

(4) If FA, FB and FC are omitted, the system considers that FA = FB = FC = F. If FB is included but
      FC omitted, it considers that FC = FB.

(5) If a single digit of F is assigned for F, FA, FB or FC, the command value of F will apply to all the
      zones. A single digit of F is only effective when it is assigned for an F command.

(6) If J is 0 or omitted, the system acts in the same manner as with G73 (i.e. cuts to the depth of I).

(7) If I  J, the system acts in the same manner as with G83 (i.e. cuts to the depth of J).

(8) For the address character used for fixed deep drilling cycle 2, the preset value will be retained
      as with other fixed cycles. When executing a fixed cycle using the same address character,
      cancel the value (e.g. G80 or G1).

(9) This instruction always needs an S command. If this instruction is given with no S command,
      alarm 2589 will be raised.

(10) If the SC command is omitted, the system considers that SC = S.

(11) If the effective gear speed is preset below the lower limit during gradual deceleration (between
      S and SC), the spindle speed is locked to the lower limit gear speed.

(12) The spindle speed is gradually decreased by internally issuing an override command (in units of
      0.1%). Therefore, there will be a difference between the commanded and actual speeds.

                                                                                                                                6103-E P-113
                                           SECTION 14 SYNCHRONIZED TAPPING FUNCTION FOR DEEP HOLES

SECTION 14 SYNCHRONIZED TAPPING FUNCTION FOR
                   DEEP HOLES

14-1. Overview

            This function is designed to perform deep hole tapping by the synchronized operation control of the
            spindle feed shaft. It is identical to the synchronized tapping function in all aspects, including
            precautions, except the cutting system.

14-2. High-speed Deep Hole Tapping Cycle

14-2-1.Command Format

          Command Format

            [G__X__Y__Z__R__E__Q__P__F__S__K__M__ ]
             G : High-speed deep hole tapping cycle command G283

                         High-speed deep hole reverse tapping cycle command G273
             X, Y : Tapping positions
             Z : Tap bottom position
             R : Point R position
             E : Point R spindle rotating position (angle)
             Q : Set the cutting depth per peck in a positive incremental value.
             P : Dwell time in the tap bottom position and the point R position
             F : Cutting feedrate
             S : Spindle speed
             K : Retract amount
             M : Spindle gear selection
                                                                                                                   6103-E P-114
                              SECTION 14 SYNCHRONIZED TAPPING FUNCTION FOR DEEP HOLES

Details of Each Command

(1) High-speed deep hole tapping cycle command
      Set this command with G283.

(2) High-speed deep hole reverse tapping cycle command
      Set this command with G273.

(3) Tapping positions (X, Y)
      Set the tapping positions.
      The last set positions will be valid when this setting is omitted.

(4) Tap bottom position (Z)
      Specify the position on the coordinate system in G90 absolute dimensioning mode, or the
      distance from the point R in G91 incremental dimensioning mode.
      This setting may be omitted but must follow the general rules of fixed cycles. (For further
      information about the general rules of fixed cycles, refer to the separate "Programming
      Manual".)

(5) Point R position (R)
      Specify the point R position.
      This setting may be omitted but must follow the general rules of fixed cycles.

(6) Point R spindle rotating position (E)
      Specify the position of tap cutting (spindle indexing position) at the start of tapping. This setting
      may be omitted but must follow the general rules of fixed cycles. That is, it is a modal command
      in fixed cycle mode. If it is omitted and has never been specified after fixed cycle mode became
      active, cutting start position control will not take place, and cutting will begin from the current
      position.

(7) Cutting depth per peck (Q)
      Set the cutting depth per peck in a positive incremental value.
      This setting may be omitted but must follow the general rules of fixed cycles.
      An alarm will occur if Q = 0 or Q < K (retract amount) (NC optional parameter long word No. 1
      when there is no K command).

(8) Dwell time in the tap bottom position and the point R position (P)
      Specify the dwell time in the tap bottom position and the point R position. It can be set in the
      same manner as G04.
      This setting may be omitted but must follow the general rules of fixed cycles.

(9) Cutting feedrate (F)
      Specify the speed of the feed shaft in the cutting feed operation area during cycle operation.
      Specify the value so that the Z-axis feed per rotation of the spindle will be the tap thread pitch.
      That is, assuming that the tap thread pitch is P and the spindle speed is S, feed-per-minute
      mode (G94) is:
          F = P·S
      In feed-per-rotation mode (G95):
          F=P
      This setting may be omitted but must follow the general rules of fixed cycles.
      An alarm will occur if an F1-digit feed command is specified.

(10) Spindle speed (S)
      Specify the spindle speed (synchronized feedrate of the spindle) during tapping.
      This setting may be omitted but must follow the general rules of fixed cycles. With this S
      command value, a gear for synchronized tapping will be automatically selected. The gear to be
                                                                                                                   6103-E P-115
                              SECTION 14 SYNCHRONIZED TAPPING FUNCTION FOR DEEP HOLES

      selected will be the same in synchronized tapping and high-speed deep hole synchronized
      tapping.

(11) Retract amount (K)
      Set the retract amount in a positive incremental value.
      This setting may be omitted but must follow the general rules of fixed cycles.
      If the retract amount has never been set, use the value of NC optional parameter long word No.
      1 for it.
      An alarm will occur if Q < K (retract amount) (NC optional parameter long word No. 1 when
      there is no K command).

(12) Spindle gear selection (M)
      A spindle gear matching the command value of the spindle speed is automatically selected
      during a tapping cycle. However, if the selected gear is improper, another spindle gear will be
      selected based on an M code.
      M234: A gear matching the spindle speed will be selected from among all gears.
      M235: The second gear will be selected even in the first gear speed range.
      M236: The third gear will be selected even in the first or second gear speed range.
      M237: The fourth gear will be selected even in the first, second, or third gear speed range.

[Supplement]

 M40 to M43 previously set will be invalid during high-speed deep hole synchronized tapping.
 They will become valid again after the cycle is canceled.
                                                                                                       6103-E P-116
                  SECTION 14 SYNCHRONIZED TAPPING FUNCTION FOR DEEP HOLES

14-2-2.Operation

High-speed Deep Hole Tapping Cycle Command (G283)

                  Operation (1)                    Return point

                                 Operation (2)                   Operation (9)

Point R                          Operation (3)                   Operation (8)
            Q                             K Operation (4)
                                                                         Rapid traverse
                                                                         Cutting feed
                                                                         (synchronized feed)

         Q                                      Operation (5)    Operation (7)

                                                K

                                                Operation (5)
                                                Operation (6)

Point Z

                                                                 ME32123R0701600040001

(1) The spindle is positioned by rapid traverse to the coordinates (X, Y) and, at the same time, a
     proper spindle gear for the S command value is selected and the spindle is deactivated.
     However, if there is an M code command within the block where the command also exists,
     these operations will be performed upon completion of positioning and execution of the M
     code command.

(2) The spindle is positioned at the point R by rapid traverse and, at the same time, the spindle
     position is positioned at point E in a single direction (rotating direction of tapping) by spindle
     indexing.

(3) The synchronized cutting feed of the spindle and the Z-axis is performed by the pecking
     amount Q in the normal rotating direction of the spindle.

(4) The synchronized cutting feed of the spindle and the Z-axis is performed by the retract amount
     K in the reverse rotating direction of the spindle.

(5) Operations (3) and (4) are repeated until point Z for machining.

(6) Dwelling is performed for P seconds whenever P is specified at point Z.

(7) The synchronized cutting feed of the spindle and the Z-axis is performed to the point R in the
     reverse rotating direction of the spindle.

(8) Dwelling is performed for P seconds whenever P is specified at the point R.
                                                                                                                   6103-E P-117
                              SECTION 14 SYNCHRONIZED TAPPING FUNCTION FOR DEEP HOLES

 (9) Positioning is performed by rapid traverse to the point of return.
       The operations (3) to (7) will be performed with the spindle override considered 100%,
       irrespective of the actual cutting feed and spindle override. Dry run will also be invalid.
       In operations (4) and (7), the return speed override for synchronized tapping will be valid.

       When the retract amount K is not set in the program, the value set at NC optional parameter
       long word No. 1 will be used.
       This parameter will also be used for G73 and G83.

High-speed Deep Hole Reverse Tapping Cycle Command (G273)

Only the rotating direction of the spindle is reversed in operations (2), (3), (4), and (7), and all other
operations are the same as those for the high-speed deep hole tapping cycle command.

Slide Hold

 (a) By pressing the SLIDE HOLD button during the operation (3), the spindle will immediately
       become deactivated, and will be synchronously fed to the point R by the reverse rotation of
       the spindle and then come to a stop.

 (b) By pressing the SLIDE HOLD button during the operation (4), the spindle will be
       synchronously fed to the point R by the reverse rotation of the spindle upon completion of the
       operation and then come to a stop.

 (c) By pressing the SLIDE HOLD button during the operation (6) or (7), the spindle will move to
       the point R and then be held.

 (d) By pressing the Start button during hold at the point R as a result of (a) to (c), the
       synchronized feed of the spindle and the Z-axis to the pecking amount Q will begin again.

[Supplement]

 Manual interruption will be invalid during hold at the point R.
                                                                                                                                6103-E P-118
                                           SECTION 14 SYNCHRONIZED TAPPING FUNCTION FOR DEEP HOLES

14-3. Deep Hole Tapping Cycle

14-3-1.Command System

          Command Format

[G__X__Y__Z__R__E__Q__(I__J__)P__F__S__K__M__ ]

G : Deep hole tapping cycle command G282
           Deep hole reverse tapping cycle command G272

X, Y : Tapping positions

Z : Tap bottom position

R : Point R position

E : Point R spindle rotating position (angle)

Q : Set the cutting depth per peck in a positive incremental value.

I  : Cutting depth per peck (When there is a Q command, it will be given priority over this

   setting.)

J : Tool tip drawing amount (When there is a Q command, it will be given priority over this
           setting.)

P : Dwell time in the tap bottom position and the point R position

F : Cutting feedrate

S : Spindle speed

K : Retract amount

M : Spindle gear selection
                                                                                                                   6103-E P-119
                              SECTION 14 SYNCHRONIZED TAPPING FUNCTION FOR DEEP HOLES

Details of Each Command

(1) Deep hole tapping cycle command
      Set this command with G282.

(2) Deep hole reverse tapping cycle command
      Set this command with G272.

(3) Tapping positions (X, Y)
      Set the tapping positions.
      The last set positions will be valid when this setting is omitted.

(4) Tap bottom position (Z)
      Specify the position on the coordinate system in G90 absolute dimensioning mode, or the
      distance from the point R in G91 incremental dimensioning mode.
      This setting may be omitted but must follow the general rules of fixed cycles. (For further
      information about the general rules of fixed cycles, refer to the separate "Programming
      Manual".)

(5) Point R position (R)
      Specify the point R position.
      This setting may be omitted but must follow the general rules of fixed cycles.

(6) Point R spindle rotating position (E)
      Specify the position of tap cutting (spindle indexing position) at the start of tapping. This setting
      may be omitted but must follow the general rules of fixed cycles. That is, it is a modal command
      in fixed cycle mode. If it is omitted and has never been specified after fixed cycle mode became
      active, cutting start position control will not take place, and cutting will begin from the current
      position.

(7) Cutting depth per peck (Q)
      Set the cutting depth per peck in a positive incremental value.
      This setting may be omitted but must follow the general rules of fixed cycles.
      An alarm will occur if |Q| < d1 (retract amount) (NC optional parameter long word No. 2).

(8) Cutting depth per peck (I)
      Set the cutting depth per peck in a positive incremental value.
      When there is a Q command, it will be given priority over this setting.
      If both I and J are 0, an alarm will occur.
      An alarm will occur if I < K (retract amount) (NC optional parameter long word No. 1 when there
      is no K command). (when | I | < | J |)

(9) Tool tip drawing amount (J)
      Set the tool tip drawing amount per peck (cutting depth until return to the point R) in a positive
      incremental value.
      When there is a Q command, it will be given priority over this setting.
      If J = 0, the same operation as G283 will take place. In this case, the cutting depth will be I.
      When | I |  | J |, J will be treated as | I |.
      An alarm will occur if | J | < d1 (retract amount) (NC optional parameter long word No. 2).

(10) Dwell time in the tap bottom position and the point R position (P)
      Specify the dwell time in the tap bottom position and the point R position. It can be set in the
      same manner as G04.
      This setting may be omitted but must follow the general rules of fixed cycles.
                                                                                                                   6103-E P-120
                              SECTION 14 SYNCHRONIZED TAPPING FUNCTION FOR DEEP HOLES

(11) Cutting feedrate (F)
      Specify the speed of the feed shaft in the cutting feed operation area during cycle operation.
      Specify the value so that the Z-axis feed per rotation of the spindle will be the tap thread pitch.
      That is, assuming that the tap thread pitch is P and the spindle speed is S, feed-per-minute
      mode (G94) is:
          F = P·S
      In feed-per-rotation mode (G95):
          F=P
      This setting may be omitted but must follow the general rules of fixed cycles.
      An alarm will occur if an F1-digit feed command is specified.

(12) Spindle speed (S)
      Specify the spindle speed (synchronized feedrate of the spindle) during tapping.
      This setting may be omitted but must follow the general rules of fixed cycles. With this S
      command value, a gear for synchronized tapping will be automatically selected. The gear to be
      selected will be the same in synchronized tapping and deep hole synchronized tapping.

(13) Retract amount (K)
      Set the retract amount in a positive incremental value.
      This setting may be omitted but must follow the general rules of fixed cycles.
      If the retract amount has never been set, use the value of NC optional parameter long word No.
      1 for it.

(14) Spindle gear selection (M)
      A spindle gear matching the command value of the spindle speed is automatically selected
      during a tapping cycle. However, if the selected gear is improper, another spindle gear will be
      selected based on an M code.
      M234: A gear matching the spindle speed will be selected from among all gears.
      M235: The second gear will be selected even in the first gear speed range.
      M236: The third gear will be selected even in the first or second gear speed range.
      M237: The fourth gear will be selected even in the first, second, or third gear speed range.

[Supplement]

 M40 to M43 previously set will be invalid during deep hole synchronized tapping. They will
 become valid again after the cycle is canceled.
                                                                                                                  6103-E P-121
                             SECTION 14 SYNCHRONIZED TAPPING FUNCTION FOR DEEP HOLES

14-3-2.Operation

Deep Hole Tapping Cycle Command (G282)

In the case of a Q command

            Operation (1)                                                  Return point

                             Operation (2)                                 Operation (11)

Point R                                                                    Operation (10)

              Operation (3)                   Operation (5)                         Rapid traverse
         Q                   Operation (4)                                          Cutting feed
                                                                                    (synchronized feed)
                                                                      (5)
                                                                           Operation (9)
                                            d1
                                                              (4)

         Q                   Operation (6)  Operation (7)

                                            d1
                                            (6)

Point Z

                                                                           Operation (8)

                                                                                          ME32123R0701600090001

(1) The spindle is positioned by rapid traverse to the coordinates (X, Y) and, at the same time, a
      proper spindle gear for the S command value is selected and the spindle is deactivated.
      However, if there is an M code command within the block where the command also exists,
      these operations will be performed upon completion of positioning and execution of the M
      code command.

(2) The spindle is positioned at the point R by rapid traverse and, at the same time, the spindle
      position is positioned at point E in a single direction (rotating direction of tapping) by spindle
      indexing.

(3) The synchronized cutting feed of the spindle and the Z-axis is performed by the pecking
      amount Q in the normal rotating direction of the spindle.

(4) The synchronized cutting feed of the spindle and the Z-axis is performed to the point R in the
      reverse rotating direction of the spindle.

(5) In the second and subsequent cutting operations, the synchronized cutting feed of the spindle
      and the Z-axis is performed by the normal rotation of the spindle in a position ahead of the last
      machining position by d1.
      However, the operation (5) will not take place when the return speed override for synchronized
      tapping is 100%.

(6) The synchronized cutting feed of the spindle and the Z-axis is performed by Q + d1 by the
      normal rotation of the spindle.

(7) Operations (4), (5), and (6) are repeated until point Z for machining.
                                                                                                                   6103-E P-122
                              SECTION 14 SYNCHRONIZED TAPPING FUNCTION FOR DEEP HOLES

 (8) Dwelling is performed for P seconds whenever P is specified at point Z.
 (9) The synchronized cutting feed of the spindle and the Z-axis is performed to the point R in the

        reverse rotating direction of the spindle.
(10) Dwelling is performed for P seconds whenever P is specified at the point R.
 (11) Positioning is performed by rapid traverse to the point of return.

        Operations (3) to (9) will be performed with the spindle override considered 100%, irrespective
        of the actual cutting feed and spindle override. Dry run will also be invalid.
        In the operations (4), (5), and (9), the return speed override function is usable for
        synchronized tapping.
        An in-position check will not be performed at the end of operation (5).

In the case of I and J commands

             Operation (1)                                         Return point

                            Operation (2)                                         Operation (14)

             I                  Operation (4)                                              Rapid traverse

(1) The spindle is positioned by rapid traverse to the coordinates (X, Y) and, at the same time, a
      proper spindle gear for the S command value is selected and the spindle is deactivated.
      However, if there is an M code command within the block where the command also exists,
      these operations will be performed upon completion of positioning and execution of the M
      code command.

(2) The spindle is positioned at the point R by rapid traverse and, at the same time, the spindle
      position is positioned at point E in a single direction (rotating direction of tapping) by spindle
      indexing.

(3) The synchronized cutting feed of the spindle and the Z-axis is performed by the cutting depth I
      in the normal rotating direction of the spindle.

(4) The synchronized cutting feed of the spindle and the Z-axis is performed by the retract amount
      K in the reverse rotating direction of the spindle.

(5) The synchronized cutting feed of the spindle and the Z-axis is performed by I + K in the normal
      rotating direction of the spindle

(6) Operations (4) and (5) are performed until the tool tip drawing amount reaches J.
                                                                                                                   6103-E P-123
                              SECTION 14 SYNCHRONIZED TAPPING FUNCTION FOR DEEP HOLES

 (7) The synchronized cutting feed of the spindle and the Z-axis is performed to the point R in the
        reverse rotating direction of the spindle.

 (8) The synchronized cutting feed of the spindle and the Z-axis is performed by the normal
        rotation of the spindle to a position ahead of the last machining position by d1.
        However, the operation (8) will not take place when the return speed override for synchronized
        tapping is 100%.

 (9) The synchronized cutting feed of the spindle and the Z-axis is performed by I + d1 by the
        normal rotation of the spindle.

(10) Operations (3) to (9) are repeated until point Z for machining.
 (11) Dwelling is performed for P seconds whenever P is specified at point Z.
(12) The synchronized cutting feed of the spindle and the Z-axis is performed to the point R in the

        reverse rotating direction of the spindle.
(13) Dwelling is performed for P seconds whenever P is specified at the point R.
(14) Positioning is performed by rapid traverse to the point of return.

        Operations (3) to (12) will be performed with the spindle override considered 100%,
        irrespective of the actual cutting feed and spindle override. Dry run will also be invalid.
        In operations (4), (7), (8), and (12), the return speed override for synchronized tapping will be
        valid.
        An in-position check will not be performed at the end of operation (8).

        For the retract amount d1, the value set at NC optional parameter long word No. 2 will be
        used.
        This parameter will also be used for G83.

        When the retract amount K is not set in the program, the value set at NC optional parameter
        long word No. 1 will be used.
        This parameter will also be used for G73 and G83.

The following operation takes place depending on the values I and J. If either I or J is set, The NC
regard that 0 is set for the other command.

a. Without Q command
      When J = 0, the same operation as G283 takes place according to the cutting depth I.
      When I  J, the same operation as G282 takes place according to the cutting depth J.
      When I < J, the operation in the case of I and J commands as described takes place.
      An alarm will occur when both I and J are 0.

b. With Q command
      The operation in the case of a Q command takes place, irrespective of the values of I and J.
                                                                                                                   6103-E P-124
                              SECTION 14 SYNCHRONIZED TAPPING FUNCTION FOR DEEP HOLES

Deep Hole Reverse Tapping Cycle Command (G272)

In the case of a Q command

Operations (3) to (6) and (9) are performed.

In the case of I and J commands

The same operations as those of the deep hole tapping cycle command (G282) are performed,
except that the rotating direction of the spindle is reversed in operations (3) to (9) and (12).

Slide Hold

In the case of a Q command

 (a) By pressing the SLIDE HOLD button during the operation (3), (5), or (6), the spindle will
       immediately become deactivated, and will be synchronously fed to the point R by the reverse
       rotation of the spindle and then come to a stop.

 (b) By pressing the SLIDE HOLD button during the operation (4) or (9), the spindle will move to
       the point R and then be held.

 (c) By pressing the Start button during hold at the point R as a result of (a) and (b), the
       synchronized feed of the spindle and the Z-axis by synchronized feed will begin.

In the case of I and J commands

 (a) By pressing the SLIDE HOLD button during the operation (3), (5), (8), or (9), the spindle will
       immediately become deactivated, and will be synchronously fed to the point R by the reverse
       rotation of the spindle and come to a stop.

 (b) By pressing the SLIDE HOLD button during the operation (4), the spindle will be
       synchronously fed to the point R by the reverse rotation of the spindle upon completion of the
       operation and then come to a stop.

 (c) By pressing the SLIDE HOLD button during the operation (7) or (12), the spindle will move to
       the point R and then be held.

 (d) By pressing the Start button during hold at the point R as a result of (a) to (c), the
       synchronized feed of the spindle and the Z-axis by synchronized feed will begin.

[Supplement]

 Manual interruption will be invalid during hold at the point R.
                                                                                                                                6103-E P-125
                                           SECTION 14 SYNCHRONIZED TAPPING FUNCTION FOR DEEP HOLES

14-4. Parameters

The high-speed deep hole tapping cycle and deep hole tapping cycle commands us the same
parameters as those for the tapping cycle commands (G284, G274).
These commands additionally use the following parameter.

When there is no program command, the value set at NC optional parameter long word No. 1 will be
used for the retract amount K.
This parameter will also be used for G73 and G83.

NC optional parameter long word No. 1

Description  : In the G73 cycle, the spindle cuts in by Q, retracts by d again, and re-cuts in
                by Q + d. Set the amount of d.
                In the G83 cycle, when the cutting depth per peck is set at I, the spindle cuts
                in by I, retracts by d again, and re-cuts in by I + d. This operation is continued
                until the tool tip drawing amount reaches J. Set the amount of d.
                In the G273 or G283 cycle, the spindle cuts in by Q, retracts by d again, and
                re-cuts in by Q + d. Set the amount of d. When a K command is set in the
                program, it will be given priority.
                In the G272 or G282 cycle, when the cutting depth per peck is set at I, the
                spindle cuts in by I, retracts by d again, and re-cuts in by I + d. This operation
                is continued until the tool tip drawing amount reaches J. Set the amount of d.
                When a K command is set in the program, it will be given priority.

Setting range : 0 to 5000

Initial value : 500

Unit         : m or 0.0001 inch

For the retract amount d1, the value set at NC optional parameter long word No. 2 will be used.
This parameter will also be used for G83.

NC optional parameter long word No. 2

Description  : In the G83 cycle, the spindle cuts in J (Q), returns to the point R, is positioned
                to a position ahead of the last cutting position by d1, and re-cuts in by I (Q) +
                d1. Set the amount of d1.
                In the G272 or G282 cycle, the spindle cuts in by Q (J), returns to the point R,
                cuts in to a position ahead of the last cutting position by d1, and re-cuts in by
                Q (I) + d1. Set the amount of d1.

Setting range : 0 to 5000

Initial value : 1000

Unit         : m or 0.0001 inch
                                                                                                                                6103-E P-126
                                           SECTION 14 SYNCHRONIZED TAPPING FUNCTION FOR DEEP HOLES

14-5. Supplement

               G282, G272, G283, and G273 are fixed cycles and canceled with the G80 command.

                   They can also be canceled with the G00, G01, G02, or G03 command.

               An F command is required whenever the G282, G272, G283, or G273 is to be executed after

                   the power is turned on, NC is reset, or a fixed cycle is canceled.

               An S command is required whenever the G282, G272, G283, or G273 is to be executed after

                   the power is turned on or NC is reset.

               Whether the spindle stops at the point R in a single block depends on the same parameter as

                   that for the tapping cycle command.
                   However, the point R is limited to that in operation (2).

               Sequence return to a midpoint of a block in internal operation is not permitted during the

                   synchronized operation of the spindle and the Z-axis.

               The synchronized feedrate in STM locking or machine locking is F specified. (The spindle is

                   considered rotating.)

            Note
            1) To execute sequence return in synchronized tapping mode, select a block that does not get

                 synchronized feed involved in return operation. (Synchronized feed may not be properly
                 executed.)
            2) Do not turn on or off the STM lock during synchronized tapping mode. (Synchronized feed may
                 not be properly executed.)

14-6. Continuous Synchronized Tapping Function

            The continuous synchronized tapping function (bit 5 of NC optional parameter bit No. 66: 1) is
            available for both of the deep hole synchronized tapping and high-speed deep hole synchronized
            tapping functions.

14-7. Synchronized Tapping Torque Monitoring Function

            Cutting operation
            The synchronized tapping torque is monitored during cutting by the spindle and the Z-axis by
            synchronized feed or cutting by the spindle and the Z-axis by synchronized feed to a position ahead
            of the last cutting position.
            It is not monitored in drawing operation.
            The setting method, parameters, etc. are the same as those for the tapping cycle command.
                                                                                                  6103-E P-127
             SECTION 14 SYNCHRONIZED TAPPING FUNCTION FOR DEEP HOLES

                            deep hole drilling cycle (G73), deep hole drilling cycle (G83), fine boring (G76) or back
                            boring (G87) command is set.

                      3) The value of Q is "0" when the high-speed deep hole (reverse) tapping cycle command
                            G283 (G273) is set.

                      4) The value of Q is "0" when the deep hole (reverse) tapping cycle command G282
                            (G272) is set. Or, the value with both I and J commands are "0".

                      4) Specify the cutting depth to Q, or the cutting depth and the tool tip drawing amount with

                            I and J.

                                                                           SECTION 15 G33 THREAD CUTTING FUNCTION

SECTION 15 G33 THREAD CUTTING FUNCTION

15-1. Overview

            The thread cutting function by G33 is used to cut threads at a specified pitch by synchronizing the
            axis feed with spindle rotation.
            The thread cutting command (G33) is executed in the unfixed cycle that belongs to G00, G01, G02,
            G60, G172, or G173 group.

15-2. Programming Command

15-2-1.Command Format

            This is the function for cutting threads at a specified pitch.

            [Command format]
            Thread cutting: G33 IP_ F_ E_ P_

             IP : Axes to be fed and their travel length
                         In the absolute dimensioning mode (G90), specify the thread end position in the selected
                         coordinate system (in work or local coordinate system).
                         In the incremental dimensioning mode (G91), specify the thread length.

             F : Thread pitch
                         Specify the axis to which this thread pitch is applied with the parameter THE CUTTING AXIS OF
                         F COMMAND IN THE G33. (For the parameter setting method, refer to 15-3. "Parameters.")
                         This instruction manual expresses the axis to which this thread pitch is applied as pitch-applied
                         axis.
                         Thread pitch of the pitch-applied axis: [F command]
                         Thread pitch of the other axes:
                         Travel distance calculated by the following formula:
                         [F command value] × [Travel distance of the axis] ÷ [Travel distance of the pitch applied axis]
                         The F command specified in G33 mode is unconditionally regarded as a feed-per-revolution
                         command (mm/rev feed). The command unit conforms to the programmed unit system.

             E : Variation of the pitch for the thread with variable pitch
                         Axis movement occurs so that the thread pitch of the pitch applied axis becomes the pitch
                         variation specified with E.
                         The pitch variation per pitch of the axis other than pitch-applied axis is as follows:
                         [E command value] × [Travel distance of the axis] ÷ [Travel distance of the pitch applied axis]
                         If the E command is omitted, the E value is regarded as 0. The E command value is effective
                         only in the G33 mode as a modal code. If G33 is cancelled, the E command value is reset to 0.
                         The command unit conforms to the programmed command system.

             P : The number of thread within the thread pitch specified with F
                         If the P command is omitted, the P value is regarded as 1.

Metric input         E command range
 Inch input   -500.000 to 500.000 [mm/rev]
              -50.0000 to 50.0000 [inch/rev]

 Both thread cutting feed F and pitch variation amount E are feed-per-revolution commands.
     Therefore, when specifying G33 command in feed-per-minute mode, be sure to specify an F
     command. Do the same also when specifying a feed-per-minute command in G33 mode.
                                                                                                                                6103-E P-130
                                                                           SECTION 15 G33 THREAD CUTTING FUNCTION

15-2-2.Relation between Spindle Speed and Thread Pitch

The spindle speed and thread pitch have the following restrictions.

[Min spindle speed]  R  [Max feedrate] ÷ [Thread pitch]

R     : Spindle speed [min-1] (rpm)

Thread pitch : mm or inch

Max feedrate : Value in mm/min or a converted value in inch/min

By the above restriction, the thread pitch is restricted as follows.

[Max feedrate] ÷ Rmin  [Thread pitch]

Rmin  : Minimum spindle speed [min-1] (rpm)

The feedrate (mm/min or inch/min) can be converted by the following formula.

F [mm/rev or inch/rev] × S [min-1] (rpm)

 If the issued command causes the feedrate [converted value in mm/min] to exceed the
      feedrate clamp value (determined by a machine limit or the setting at the NC optional
      parameter long word No.10), the thread pitch will deviate from the specified pitch. This is
      because the feedrate is limited to the clamped value.
      When the feedrate is clamped, the following message appears:
      4204 Alarm D Feedrate command limit over (replacing)

15-2-3.Thread Cutting Program Example

With the program shown below, the tread is cut in G33 mode, the spindle is directed in a certain
direction with M19 (on assumption that the cutting edge is directed in negative X-axis direction).
Then, the tool is retracted in positive X-axis and positive Z-axis directions.

                     G90 G0 X10.Y10.Z15.S50 M3
                     G33 Z-15.F2.
                     M19

                     G0 X15.
                               Z15.
                                                                                     6103-E P-131
                                 SECTION 15 G33 THREAD CUTTING FUNCTION

setting mode.

                                                                                                                                                                                           ME32123R0701700050001

THE CUTTING AXIS OF F1 COMMAND IN THE G33

Specify the axis to which the thread pitch F is applied (pitch-applied axis) in G33 mode.

                  Initial value  Longest move

When the LONGEST MOVE is selected, the axis with the longest travel distance in the block where
G33 is specified is regarded as the pitch-applied axis.

This parameter can be changed in the following procedure:

Procedure :

  1 Select [F1] (MENU) from the function menu.

  2 Select the function key of the axis that you want to set as a pitch-applied axis from the pop-up

      function menu.

                                                                                                                                                              ME32123R0701700050002

 It is possible to select 4th, 5th, or even 6th axis regardless of the machine specifications.
      However, if the selected axis is not found during axis movement in G33 mode, the
      following alarm occurs in program execution:
      Alarm B 2253 Data word: axis command
                                                                                                                                6103-E P-132
                                                                           SECTION 15 G33 THREAD CUTTING FUNCTION

15-4. Notes for Using Thread Cutting Function

               In G33 mode, the spindle override or the feedrate override is invalid (fixed to 100%).

               The NC slide hold is invalid during operation in G33 mode. However, the RUN lamp goes out

                   and the SLIDE HOLD button lamp blinks, indicating that the SLIDE HOLD button is pressed and
                   the machine will stop after completing the current G33 block.
                   At this time, the RUN lamp and the SLIDE HOLD lamp go out.

               The dry run feed is effective as usual during thread cutting cycle, and the slide hold during dry

                   run is normally effective. In the dry run, however, the axis moves by ignoring E command during
                   thread cutting cycle. Therefore, if the DRY RUN switch is turned on and then turned OFF in a
                   thread cutting block, the operation according to the variable pitch command will not be
                   guaranteed.

               If a rotary axis command is issued in thread cutting mode (G33 mode), an alarm occurs.

                   2359 Alarm B Data word: rotary axis command 1

               During thread cutting mode (G33 mode), F1-digit command cannot be issued. If attempted, the

                   following alarm occurs:
                   2244 Alarm B Data word "F" 2

               With the continuous thread-cutting command, the thread cutting function joins the threads

                   smoothly. In the following cases, however, the threads are not joined smoothly.

                    Thread cutting command was issued in the MDI mode.

                    The previous operation has been done in other than G33 mode (G04, G00 to G03, G22, or

                         G94).

                    The dry run feed command was issued during thread cutting operation. The block that

                         comes immediately after such command will fail to created smooth joint.

                    The program was interrupted by slide-hold or single block command between the thread

                         cutting blocks. The latter block will fail to create smooth joint.

                    The block that comes immediately after the sequence restart fails to create smooth joint.

               The axis feed is accelerated or decelerated near the thread cutting start or end point; thereby,

                   some of the threads are not cut at the specified pitch. The affected time is about 50 ms each at
                   start and end points. In order to cut at the specified pitch, secure the approach distance when
                   starting thread cutting.

               During machine lock or STM lock, an estimated spindle speed is internally generated only in

                   M03 or M04 mode. The estimated spindle speed conforms to the setting at the NC optional
                   parameter word No. 9. The setting unit is [min-1] (rpm).

               If 0 (zero) travel distance is set for the pitch-applied axis in the thread cutting command, the

                   following alarm occurs when the command block is executed.
                   2253 Alarm B Data word: Axis command
                   Code: Value set for the pitch-applied axis

               In the tool shape setting, the tool shapes are not correlated with the thread cutting tools.

                   Therefore, in G33 thread cutting, the real 3D display draws a figure equivalent to the figure for
                   normal cutting feed. But the 3D display does not draw a thread shape.
                                                                          6103-E P-133

15-5-3.Alarm D

           4204 Feedrate command limit over (replacing)

                     This is a warning alarm indicating that the F command value is being replaced with the upper
                     limit so that the F command value (including override) does not exceed the allowable
                     feedrate.
                     The allowable feedrate refers to the smallest one among the following feedrates:
                     - Maximum feedrate set at the NC optional parameter (long word No.10)
                     - Machine-specific maximum cutting feedrate (set by Okuma)
                     - Upper limit feedrate in the tolerance control mode (effective only in the tolerance control
                     mode)

                     [Index]
                     None

                     [Character-string]
                     None

                     [Code]
                     The F command value being replaced in hexadecimal (unit: 0.001 mm, 6.4 ms)

                     [Probable Faulty Locations]
                     1 The F command value is too large. (The unit system may be wrongly set.)
                     2 A too small value is set as the maximum feedrate.
                     3 Override rate is tool large.

                     [Measures to Take]
                     Review the F command.
                     Increase the maximum feedrate setting.
                     Select a smaller override rate.
                                                                                                                                6103-E P-136
                                           SECTION 16 TOOL NOSE INCOMPLETE PORTION OFFSET FUNCTION

SECTION 16 TOOL NOSE INCOMPLETE PORTION
                   OFFSET FUNCTION

16-1. Overview

            A tool nose has a sharp edge (incomplete portion, represented by REF). Since the tool position is
            offset based on the tool length offset amount "H" a hole cannot be machined to the correct depth if
            the hole depth is specified in the program using the value found in the drawing; the hole is machined
            to the depth shallower than the REF amount. To solve this problem, the tool must be offset by the
            value, obtained by subtracting the REF amount from the tool length offset amount "H".
            The number of REF data sets matches the number of H command data sets, and the REF data of
            the same REF data number as the tool offset number "H" is used for offset.

16-2. Setting Tool Data

16-2-1.Offset of Tool Edge Incomplete Portion

            Procedure :

           1 Select the TOOL DATA mode.

                   Select [F8] (DISPLAY CHANGE) in the function menu.
                   The display change popup window opens.
                   In this window, select TOOL REF OFFSET and the TOOL REF OFFSET screen is displayed.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                       ME32123R0704900020001

                                         Fig.16-1 TOOL DATA - TOOL REF OFFSET Screen (Page 1)
                                                                                                                6103-E P-137
                           SECTION 16 TOOL NOSE INCOMPLETE PORTION OFFSET FUNCTION

                                                                                                                                                                                       ME32123R0704900020002

                       Fig.16-2 TOOL DATA - TOOL REF OFFSET Screen (Last Page)

2 One page of the TOOL REF OFFSET display screen shows 40 sets of the REF offset data.

    If the required data number is not found in the displayed page, change the display page by
    pressing the PAGE key or by selecting [F4] (FIND) in the function menu.
    If you change the display page by pressing the [PAGE] key, locate the cursor on the desired
    data using the cursor keys.

3 Input the REF offset data, following the same procedure as inputting the tool length offset data.

    When entering the TOOL REF OFFSET for the TOOL DATA 1st tool length offset amount (HA),
    2nd tool length offset amount (HB) and 3rd tool length offset amount (HC), first start with the
    [F2] (TOOL DATA EDIT) on the TOOL DATA screen.
                                                                                                                                6103-E P-138
                                           SECTION 16 TOOL NOSE INCOMPLETE PORTION OFFSET FUNCTION

16-3. Offset Function

16-3-1.Tool Length Offset and Tool REF Offset

            This function corrects the tool position so that the tool nose comes to the programmed position.
            Command format: {G54 to G59} H_
            When the command as shown above is given, the tool position is offset (tool length offset) by the
            value as shown below which is calculated from the tool length offset value (H) and the tool nose
            incomplete portion offset value (REF).

               = H - REF
            The offset number specified by "H" is used to call the offset data H and REF.

           Used G codes

G code             Function

  G53   Tool length offset: Cancel
  G54   Tool length offset: X-axis
  G55   Tool length offset: Y-axis
  G56   Tool length offset: Z-axis
  G57   Tool length offset: 4th axis
  G58   Tool length offset: 5th axis
  G59   Tool length offset: 6th axis

Command format

{G54 to G59} H_

H: Specify the tool offset number to call the tool length offset data.
       Designation is possible in the following range:
       H00, H1 - H999, HA, HB, HC

       H00: Offset data is "0".

       HA: When the active tool number is 1 to 999, the offset amount is taken from the offset
               number with the same number as the active tool number. When the active tool number
               is 1000 or over, the offset amount is the 1st tool length offset amount set for the TOOL
               DATA.

       HB: The offset amount is the 2nd tool length offset amount set for the TOOL DATA of the
               active tool number.

       HC: The offset amount is the 3rd tool length offset amount set for the TOOL DATA of the
               active tool number.

                                           SECTION 16 TOOL NOSE INCOMPLETE PORTION OFFSET FUNCTION

16-4. System Variables

It is possible to read and write the TOOL REF OFFSET using system variables.

     System                  Subscript          Setting Range                 Remarks
Variable Name  i:1 to 999 (Tool Offset Number)

               i:1 to 99999999 (Tool Number)                                 H999 on the [TOOL REF

                10002(HB)                       -999.999 to 999.999 Used for read/write
                10003(HC)                                                    TOOL REF OFFSET
                                                                             (HA/HB/HC) for each of
                                                                             the tools

Tool length offset amount (VTOFH[i],VTOHT[i,j]) reading/writing

 When reading VTOFH[i]

   Read the offset amount of the Tool Offset Number set at [i]. VTOFR[i] is not taken into
   consideration.

 When writing VTOFH[i]

   Set the (set value) for the offset amount of the Tool Offset Number set at [i]. The value of
   VTOFR[i] is not changed.

       VTOFH[i] = (set value)

 When reading VTOHT[i,j]

   For the Tool Number set at [i], read the difference between the shape amount (VTOHG[i,j]) of
   the Tool Offset Number set at [j] and the Tool REF Offset amount.

       VTOHT[i,j] = VTOHG[i,j] - VTOHR[i,j]

 When writing VTOHT[i,j]

   The operation differs according to the state of the NC optional parameter bit No.79 bit1.

Not checked    : For the Tool Number set at [i], set the (set value) for the shape amount
                 (VTOHG[i,j]) of the Tool Offset Number set at [j], and set the Tool REF Offset
                 amount to [0].
                     VTOHG[i,j] = (set value)
                     VTOHR[i,j] = 0

Checked        : For the Tool Number set at [i], the value of the shape amount (VTOHG[i,j]) of
                 the Tool Offset Number set at [j] is not changed, and write the result of the
                 expression bellow to the Tool REF Offset amount.
                     VTOHG[i,j] = no change
                     VTOHR[i,j] = VTOHG[i,j] - (set value)

                                                                           SECTION 17 INTERRUPT PROGRAM FUNCTION

SECTION 17 INTERRUPT PROGRAM FUNCTION

17-1. Overview

            With this function, the interrupt program ON signal (hereinafter called as "signal") is turned off to
            execute the other program (interrupt program) specified after then.
            When the execution of the interrupt program starts, modal commands issued so far are reset. When
            the execution of the interrupt program is finished, a reset processing is performed at the M02/M30
            level and the execution of the part programs (main program and subprogram) are quitted.
                                                                                          6103-E P-142
                                      SECTION 17 INTERRUPT PROGRAM FUNCTION

17-2. Description

The interrupt validation command (M901 to M905) is given during the execution of a part program to
execute an interrupt program (O901 to O905) when the signal is turned off.

The interrupt program is stored in the exclusive interrupt program file (A. INT), and loaded to the
operation buffer when the power supply is turned on.
The interrupt program has a format beginning with program O901 (or O902 to O905) and ending
with an RTI command.

(Example) A. INT                      ........  Standby up to the positive limit
       O901                           ........  Travel to the tool change position.
       G0 Z=VPSLZ-VZOFZ[VACOD]-10     ........  Spindle stop
       G30 P1                         ........  Coolant group OFF
       M05                                      1244 Alarm A User reserve code 1
       M09                            ........  `EMERGENCY STOP'
       VUACM[1]='''EMERGENCY STOP'''
       VDOUT[993]=1
       M00
       RTI

[Supplement]

 1) When an interrupt program invalidation command (M900) is issued, even if the signal is turned
      off, the interrupt program is not executed.

 2) When alarm A has been issued, the NC system is reset, or the machine is in the manual
      operation mode, even if the signal is turned off, the interrupt program is not executed.

 3) If the signal is turned on or off after the interrupt program starts operation, nothing occurs.

 4) The interrupt program starts operation only in the automatic operation mode. In the MDI
      operation mode, the interrupt program is not executed, and the alarm "4285 Alarm D Interrupt
      Program" is issued.

 5) Under the following conditions, the interrupt program is executed after the block being
      executed is completed.

        During a fixed cycle (G74, G84, G76, or G87)

        During T/M command

        During RS, RH, RI, RP, NP, RM, WP, or TN command

 6) When the signal is turned off during a synchronous tapping cycle (G274 or G284), the spindle
      is promptly stopped and reversely rotated up to the point R in the synchronous traverse, and
      then the interrupt program is executed after the spindle reaches the point R.

 7) The size of the interrupt program is 1024 bytes maximum.

 8) The RTI command is executed to terminate the interrupt program, reset at the M02/M30 level,
      and finish the main program. (No alarms are reset upon the execution of RTI command.)

 9) After the interrupt program RTI command is executed in the execution of the schedule
      program, operation follows a parameter (NC optional parameter bit No. 12 bit 7). (It is limited
      to a library program or a manufacturer subprogram.)

 10)A subprogram call command (CALL or MODIN) can be issued from the interrupt program,
      multiplicity is however allowed up to eight-fold. If this is exceeded, alarm B is issued.

 11)When the interrupt program is being executed and the tool diameter is compensated, if the
      RTI command is issued without a tool diameter compensation cancel command (G40), alarm
      B is issued.
                                                                                                 6103-E P-143
                                             SECTION 17 INTERRUPT PROGRAM FUNCTION

                        Table 1-1 NC Optional Parameter bit

     No.                                                                        setting

              The interrupt program RTI      The execution of The execution of      0
