SECTION 42 SOFTWARE EMERGENCY LIMIT
                   FUNCTION

42-1. Overview

            This function checks the limit using current positions (APA). When the limit is exceeded, an alarm A
            is raised to shut off the power of the unit, stopping the axis movement.

42-2. Function Detail

42-2-1.PARAMETER Screen

          Emergency Limit screen

            Select the PARAMETER screen and then the EMERGENCY LIMIT screen, where the parameters
            for P EMERGENCY LIMIT and N EMERGENCY LIMIT can be set.

                                                                                                                   ME32123R0704800020001

                   Fig.42-1 Emergency Limit screen

            Item   Min.value          Max.value
P EMERGENCY LIMIT         -99999.999          99999.999
N EMERGENCY LIMIT         -99999.999          99999.999

 The setting range with 0.1 m specification is from -99999.9999 to 99999.9999.
                                                                                                                   6103-E P-298
                                                 SECTION 42 SOFTWARE EMERGENCY LIMIT FUNCTION

   When both P and N EMERGENCY LIMITs are set to "0.000" ("0.0000" with 0.1 m

      specification), the limit is checked using the P/N travel limit setting value ± shift amount.
      When shifted along a linear axis: P travel limit + 1.0mm N travel limit -1.0mm
      For a rotation axis with a limit: P travel limit +1 degree N travel limit -1 degree

   Set a value adding 0.5 mm or more to the P/N travel limit by considering a possible overshoot

      triggered with an axis stop when the emergency limit is exceeded.

Parameter input/output

The P/N EMERGENCY LIMIT can be input, output, and verified.
P401
Q1...P EMERGENCY LIMIT
Q2...N EMERGENCY LIMIT

Releasing the software emergency limit

When a current position (APA) exceeds the software emergency limit range, the position can be
moved into the software emergency limit range using the following procedure.

                         Operation                                         Remarks
(When a current position has exceeded the
software emergency limit)                        The alarm A "Emergency limit over" is raised.
Press the Emergency Release button.              The axis movement stops.

Select the manual operation mode.                In the Auto or MDI operation mode,
Move the axis into the software emergency limit  the alarm A "Emergency limit being released" is
range by using the pulse handle.                 raised.
                                                 In the manual operation mode,
                                                 the alarm D "Emergency limit being released" is
                                                 raised.

                                                 While the Emergency Release button is ON,
                                                 manual operation other than pulse handle is
                                                 disabled.

                                                                                        SECTION 43 SERVONAVI M FUNCTION

SECTION 43 SERVONAVI M FUNCTION

43-1. Overview

            The workpiece weight and machine condition are estimated using predefined dedicated axis
            movements, and servo control parameters are changed to their optimum values based on the
            estimated workpiece weight and machine condition. The previously set servo control parameters are
            automatically selected when the power is turned ON.
            Furthermore, by selecting the machining application from "PARTS" or "DIE/MOLD", the optimum
            servo control parameters for the respective application are selected.

43-2. SERVONAVI M Menu Screen

            A display change pop-up window is displayed by selecting function key [F8] (DISPLAY CHANGE) at
            the operation screen function menu, allowing "SERVONAVI M" to be selected.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                      ME32123R0705000020001

                                                     Fig.43-1 SERVONAVI M Menu Screen
             * Certain models do not display the "MODE" display or function key [F6] (SELECT MODE), the

                 "WORK WEIGHT AUTO SETTING" icon or function key [F1] (WORK WT. AUTO SET), the
                 "WORK WEIGHT MANUAL SETTING" icon or function key [F2] (WORK WT. MANU SET), the
                 "REVERSAL SPIKE AUTO ADJUSTMENT" icon or function key [F3] (R. SPIKE ADJUST), and
                 the "VIBRATION AUTO ADJUSTMENT" icon or function key [F4] (VIB. ADJUST).
                                                                                                                                6103-E P-301
                                                                                        SECTION 43 SERVONAVI M FUNCTION

43-2-1.Selecting the Machining Application

            Select whether to use "PARTS" or "DIE/MOLD" for the machining application at the SERVONAVI M
            menu screen.
            The procedure is as follows.
            The machining application cannot be selected on certain models.

                                                              ME32123R0705000030001

                         Fig.43-2 Mode selection screen

(1) Press function key [F6] (SELECT MODE) to display a pop-up window allowing [F1] (PARTS),
      [F2] (DIE/MOLD), or [F8] (CANCEL) to be selected.

(2) Select the machining application based on the workpiece.

(3) If the selected machining application differs from the previous setting,
      "4217 ALARM-D Power ON effective parameter set" appears.

(4) Reboot the machine.

(5) Servo control parameters to match the selected machining application are selected.
                                                                                                                                6103-E P-302
                                                                                        SECTION 43 SERVONAVI M FUNCTION

43-2-2.Selecting the Functions to be Used

            Decide whether to use the workpiece weight auto setting, workpiece weight manual setting, reversal
            spike auto adjustment, or vibration auto adjustment function at the SERVONAVI M menu screen.
            The functions that can be selected will differ depending on the model. Functions that cannot be
            selected do not appear on the screen.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                      ME32123R0705000040001

                                                     Fig.43-3 SERVONAVI M menu screen

               With the workpiece weight auto setting, the workpiece weight is estimated using predefined

                   dedicated axis movements, and the servo control parameters are changed to optimum values
                   based on the estimated weight.
                   Press function key [F1] (WORK WT. AUTO SET) or the "WORK WEIGHT AUTO SETTING"
                   icon to change to the workpiece weight auto setting screen. (See 43-3. Workpiece Weight Auto
                   Setting Screen.)

               With the workpiece weight manual setting, the workpiece weight is set directly, and the servo

                   control parameters are changed to optimum values based on the set weight.
                   Press function key [F2] (WORK WT. MANU SET) or the "WORK WEIGHT MANUAL SETTING"
                   icon to change to the workpiece weight manual setting screen. (See 43-4. Workpiece Weight
                   Manual Setting Screen.)

               With reversal spike auto adjustment, the spike amount (reversal spike) when performing a

                   reversal movement in the travel direction is measured using predefined dedicated axis
                   movements, and the servo control parameters are changed to optimum values based on the
                   measured reversal spike.
                   Press function key [F3] (R. SPIKE ADJUST) or the "REVERSAL SPIKE AUTO ADJUSTMENT"
                   icon to change to the reversal spike auto adjustment screen. (See 43-5. Reversal Spike Auto
                   Adjustment Screen.)
                                                                                                                6103-E P-303
                                                                        SECTION 43 SERVONAVI M FUNCTION

 With vibration auto adjustment, the vibration frequency is specified using predefined dedicated

   axis movements, and the servo control parameters are changed to optimum values based on
   the specified vibration frequency.
   Press function key [F4] (VIB. ADJUST) or the "VIBRATION AUTO ADJUSTMENT" icon to
   change to the vibration auto adjustment screen. (See 43-6. Vibration Auto Adjustment Screen.)

            appears.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                      ME32123R0705000050001

                                                Fig.43-4 Workpiece weight auto setting screen
            With the workpiece weight auto setting, the workpiece weight is estimated using predefined
            dedicated axis movements, and the servo control parameters are changed to optimum values based
            on the estimated weight.
            If function key [F8] (END) is pressed, the display returns to the SERVONAVI M menu screen.
            (The "WORK WEIGHT" unit is displayed in tons, however, there are times when it is displayed in
            100 kg (220 lb).)
                                                                                                                                6103-E P-305
                                                                                        SECTION 43 SERVONAVI M FUNCTION

43-3-1.Workpiece Weight Auto Setting Operation

            The procedure up to updating the servo control parameters with the workpiece weight auto setting is
            as follows.

            (1) Retract the Z-axis (or Y-axis (*1)) to the plus limit.

            (2) Set the cutting feed override to 100%.

            (3) Press function key [F1] (START) in MDI operation mode.
                   By pressing function key [F1] (START), a command used to estimate the workpiece weight set
                   in "G448 CONDITION" is sent to the MDI operation buffer.
                    * A reset is performed before sending the command to the MDI operation buffer, and
                        therefore caution is required.

                    * Refer to "43-3-2. Specifying G448 Operating Conditions" for details on G448 operating
                        conditions.

            (4) By pressing the START button, estimation is started and axis travel is performed.
                   By pressing the START button, the axis travels back and forth twice in the plus direction from
                   the current position by the distance set for "TRAVEL" at the speed set for "FEED RATE".

            (5) The servo control parameters are updated based on the estimated workpiece weight.

            (6) After update of the servo control parameters is complete, the estimated workpiece weight
                   appears on the screen, and "AUTO SET" appears to the right of the unit.
                   Furthermore, CONTROL PARA is changed to "WORK WEIGHT ADAPTIVE PARA".

            Note
            1) If the Z-axis (or Y-axis (*1)) is on the inside of the plus limit, or the cutting feed override has not

                 been set to 100%, an alarm occurs when starting workpiece weight estimation.
            2) Workpiece weight estimation with the workpiece weight auto setting does not measure the

                 weight of the workpiece.
                 The workpiece weight displayed on the screen is merely an approximation, and is a calculated
                 data in the course of selecting optimum servo control parameters.
            3) If the estimated workpiece weight exceeds the maximum load set beforehand at the machine,
                 the workpiece weight displayed on the screen will be the maximum load.
                 Furthermore, the servo control parameters will be updated based on the maximum load.

             (*1) The axis to be moved to the positive limit varies depending on the machine. Generally the
                    spindle should be moved to the machine upper limit in the vertical direction. With some
                    horizontal machining centers, the Z-axis should be moved to the positive limit.

            [Supplement]

              If the estimated workpiece weight exceeds the maximum load set beforehand at the machine, an
              alarm occurs.
              Even if this alarm occurs, the servo control parameters will be updated based on the maximum
              load, allowing use of the machine to be continued.

            Note
            If continuing to use the machine when overloaded to the extent that the above alarm occurs, there
            is a danger that the machine may be damaged, or that a DIFF over alarm will occur.
                                                                                                                                6103-E P-306
                                                                                        SECTION 43 SERVONAVI M FUNCTION

43-3-2.Specifying G448 Operating Conditions

            <<When the travel axis cannot be selected>>
            The command used to estimate the workpiece weight is displayed in "G448 CONDITION".
            By pressing function key [F6] (CHANGE COND.), the amount that the axis travels when estimating
            the workpiece weight can be changed.
            The optimum feed rate is automatically determined from the travel amount, and therefore "FEED
            RATE" cannot be changed directly.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                      ME32123R0705000070001

                             Fig.43-5 Workpiece weight auto setting screen: Screen when [F6] pressed
            The amount that the axis travels when estimating the workpiece weight can be changed here.
            If the amount of travel is changed, the feed rate is automatically changed based on the changed
            travel amount.
            When doing so, the "TRAVEL" and "FEED RATE" values are updated the together with the G448
            operating conditions.
            If function key [F6] (INIT. COND.) is pressed, the values change to the amount of travel and feedrate
            set before the machine was shipped.
            If function key [F8] (CANCEL) is pressed, the travel amount is not changed, and the display returns
            to the previous screen.
                                                                                                                   6103-E P-307
                                                                           SECTION 43 SERVONAVI M FUNCTION
<<When the travel axis can be selected>>
G448 CONDITION column indicates the commands used for workpiece weight estimating. The axis
to be moved at workpiece weight estimating and the travel amount can be changed by pressing [F6]
(CHANGE COND.). The estimated result is applied to all the axes used for workpiece weight
estimating.
The feedrate adopts the optimal value considering the travel distance automatically; and cannot be
changed directly.

                                                                                                                                                                                            ME32123R0705000070002

Fig.43-6 The automatic workpiece weight setting screen displayed when the travel axis is selected:
                                                After the F6 key is pressed.

To change the travel axis, move the cursor to the target axis on the display using the cursor keys 
and . When the travel axis is changed, the feedrate is changed automatically according to the
changed travel axis. At this time, the travel amount, feedrate and G448 conditions on the screen are
also updated.
The axes that can be used with workpiece weight estimating are displayed on the F1 and F2 keys.
The travel axis is changed by pressing the target axis function key.
If [F8] (END) is pressed, the screen returns to the original one without changing the travel axis.
To change the travel amount, move the cursor to the target travel amount on the display using the
cursor keys  and . When the travel amount is changed, the feedrate is changed automatically
according to the changed travel amount. At this time, the travel amount, feedrate and G448
conditions on the screen are also updated.
When [F6] (INIT. PARA) is pressed, the travel amount and feedrate are changed to the values set in
the parameter in the factory.
If [F8] (END) is pressed, the screen returns to the original one without changing the travel amount.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                      ME32123R0705000090001

            parameters are changed to optimum values based on the set workpiece weight.

                                                                                                                                6103-E P-310
                                                                                        SECTION 43 SERVONAVI M FUNCTION

43-4-1.Workpiece Weight Manual Setting Operation

            The procedure up to updating the servo control parameters with the workpiece weight manual
            setting is as follows.

            (1) Press function key [F1] (SET) and set the weight of the workpiece to be machined.
                   By setting the workpiece weight, "MANUAL SET" appears to the right of the unit.
                    * If the set workpiece weight exceeds the maximum load, an error occurs and the workpiece
                        weight is discarded.

                    * "*" (initial value) cannot be set.

            (2) "4336 ALARM-D Reset effective parameter set" appears.

            (3) Reset the machine or reboot the power.

            (4) The servo control parameters are updated based on the set workpiece weight.

            (5) After update of the servo control parameters is complete, CONTROL PARA is changed to
                   "WORK WEIGHT ADAPTIVE PARA".

43-4-2.Initial Parameters

            By pressing function key [F6] (INIT. PARA), the initial parameters can be set for the servo control
            parameters.
            The initial parameters setting procedure is as follows.

            (1) Press function key [F6] (INIT. PARA).
                   By pressing [F6] (INIT. PARA), "*" (initial value) is entered for the workpiece weight.
                   If characters are displayed to the right of the workpiece weight unit at this time, the display is
                   cleared.

            (2) "4336 ALARM-D Reset effective parameter set" appears.

            (3) Reset the machine or reboot the power.

            (4) The initial parameters are set for the servo control parameters.

            (5) After setting of the initial parameters for the servo control parameters is complete, CONTROL
                   PARA is changed to "INITIAL PARA".

            [Supplement]

              The servo control initial parameters are common to both the "PARTS" and "DIE/MOLD"
              machining applications, and the maximum load set at the machine beforehand is moved at the
              maximum acceleration that the motor is capable of.
              This is the equivalent of setting "0" or a weight exceeding the maximum load at the previous
              "Workpiece weight selection function".
              Consequently, when performing die/mold machining in particular, where high-precision machining
              is required, it is recommended that the workpiece weight be estimated by workpiece weight
              automatic setting, or set directly with workpiece weight manual setting.
                                                                                                                   6103-E P-311
                                                                           SECTION 43 SERVONAVI M FUNCTION

Note

With "SERVONAVI M", acceleration is always selected based on the set workpiece weight.
For example, if "0" is set for the workpiece weight with manual setting, the system determines that
there is either no workpiece loaded, or that the workpiece is sufficiently light, and the lowest
acceleration is selected.
Operation differs from the previous "Workpiece weight selection function", and so caution is
advised.
                                                                                                                                6103-E P-312
                                                                                        SECTION 43 SERVONAVI M FUNCTION

43-5. Reversal Spike Auto Adjustment Screen

            By pressing function key [F3] (R. SPIKE ADJUST) or the "REVERSAL SPIKE AUTO
            ADJUSTMENT" icon at the SERVONAVI M menu screen, the reversal spike auto adjustment screen
            appears.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                      ME32123R0705000120001

                                               Fig.43-9 Reversal spike auto adjustment screen
            With reversal spike auto adjustment, the reversal spike is measured using predefined dedicated axis
            movements, and the servo control parameters are changed to optimum values according to the
            measured reversal spike.
            If function key [F8] (END) is pressed, the display returns to the SERVONAVI M menu screen.
                                                                                                                                6103-E P-313
                                                                                        SECTION 43 SERVONAVI M FUNCTION

43-5-1.Reversal Spike Auto Adjustment Operation

            The procedure up to updating the servo control parameters with reversal spike auto adjustment is as
            follows.

            (1) Set the rapid traverse override and cutting feed override to 100%.

            (2) Press function key [F1] (START) in MDI operation mode.
                   By pressing function key [F1] (START), the command used to measure the spike reversal set at
                   "G458 CONDITION" is sent to the MDI operation buffer.
                    * A reset is performed before sending the command to the MDI operation buffer, and
                        therefore caution is required.

                    * Refer to "43-5-2. Specifying G458 Operating Conditions" for details on G458 operating
                        conditions.

            (3) By pressing the [START] button, measurement is started at the selected operation plane, and
                   axis travel is performed. "RUNNING" lights up adjacent to "CONTROL PARA" for the axes for
                   which reversal spike auto adjustment is being performed.
                   Details of axis travel are given below.
                   The 1st and 2nd axes in the following description refer to the X-axis and Y-axis, respectively,
                   when the X-Y plane is selected, the Z-axis and X-axis when the Z-X plane is selected, and Y-
                   axis and Z-axis when the Y-Z plane is selected.

                   a. Both the 1st and 2nd axes make a return journey in the plus direction, with the shorter
                         travel distance only from among the straight line travel L1 values, and at the lower feed rate
                         from among the straight line feed rate F1 values.

                   b. Both the 1st and 2nd axes make an outward journey in the plus direction with straight line
                         travel distance of L2 (fixed at 100 mm) only, and at the lower feed rate from among the
                         straight line feed rate F2 values.

                   c. The 1st axis travels 50 mm only in the plus direction.

                   d. Four 100 mm arc movements are repeated in the clockwise direction for each of the feed
                         rates Fs to Fe, and then the axis travels the 90° movement only a 100 mm arc
                         movement.

                   e. The 2nd axis travels 50 mm only in the minus direction, and then returns to the position
                         prior to reversal spike auto adjustment.

            (4) The servo control parameters are updated based on the measured reversal spike.

            (5) After the servo control parameters have been updated, the "RUNNING" lamp adjacent to
                   "CONTROL PARA" for the axis for which reversal spike auto adjustment was performed turns
                   off and "COMPLETE" lights up.

            Note
            1) If the rapid traverse override and cutting feed override are not set to 100%, an alarm will occur

                 when starting reversal spike measurement.
            2) A plane command is specified. If continuing with machining, specify the command for the plane

                 required for machining again.
            3) The machining fold condition may differ before and after reversal spike auto adjustment.
                                                                                                                   6103-E P-314
                                                                           SECTION 43 SERVONAVI M FUNCTION

[Supplement]

 1) The axes for which adjustment is performed are the axes displayed in the "ADJUSTABLE
      AXIS" field below the currently selected plane.

 2) Feed rates Fs to Fe are set in the machine beforehand. An n number of feed rates are set as
      shown below:
      F1, F2, Fn with Fs=F1 and Fe=Fn The n value changes depending on the machine model.

 3) With regards to axis travel 4. above, axes move only for a fixed time (*1) at feed rate Fs to Fe
      only before and after travel direction reversal relating to measurement, and at other than
      before and after travel direction reversal not relating to measurement, axes move at the
      specified feed rate (*2).
      *1 Value ([ms]) set in NC optional parameter word No. 122
      *2 Value ([mm/min]) set in NC optional parameter word No. 123

 4) The "COMPLETE" indication disappears when exiting the SERVONAVI M reversal spike auto
      adjustment screen.
                                                                                                                                6103-E P-315
                                                                                        SECTION 43 SERVONAVI M FUNCTION

43-5-2.Specifying G458 Operating Conditions

            The command used to perform reversal spike measurement is displayed in "G458 CONDITION".
            By pressing function key [F6] (CHANGE COND.), the plane for which axis travel is performed when
            measuring the reversal spike can be changed.
            The optimum value for each plane is automatically determined for the straight line travel distance
            and feed rate, and arc radius and feed rate, and therefore the values cannot be changed directly.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                      ME32123R0705000140001

                           Fig.43-10 Reversal spike auto adjustment screen: Screen when [F6] pressed
            Here, the plane for which axis travel is performed when measuring the reversal spike can be
            changed. The axes for which adjustment is performed are determined based on the changed plane.
            The axes for which adjustment is performed are the axes displayed in the "ADJUSTABLE AXIS"
            field below the plane.
            If the plane is changed, the axes, straight line travel distance and feed rate, and arc radius and feed
            rate are changed automatically based on the changed plane.
            Consequently, the axis names, straight line travel distance and feed rate, and arc radius and feed
            rate, and G458 operations displayed on the screen are also changed.
            If function key [F1] (CANCEL) is pressed, the plane is not changed, and the display returns to the
            previous screen.
            [Supplement]

              The planes that can be selected are determined beforehand based on the machine model.
              Planes that cannot be selected are grayed out, and the planes are not changed even when
              pressing the applicable function key.

                        SECTION 43 SERVONAVI M FUNCTION

Operating codes indicate how axes move while measuring reversal spike.
By pressing function key [F2] (CODE ON/OFF), the movement of axes that travel when measuring
spike reversal can be verified.

              Fig.43-11 Reversal spike auto adjustment screen: Screen when [F2] pressed

By viewing this screen during reversal spike measurement, the travel command for the axis
currently operating lights up in yellow.

[Supplement]

 Only part of the reversal spike measurement operation appears on the screen at one time. Axis
 travel commands displayed on the screen change as reversal spike measurement progresses.

Note
The reversal spike measurement operation appears in the operating code screen in program
format to make it easy to understand, however, this does not mean that reversal spike
measurement can be performed by executing the same program as that displayed on the screen.

                                                                                        SECTION 43 SERVONAVI M FUNCTION

43-6. Vibration Auto Adjustment Screen

            By pressing function key [F4] (VIB. ADJUST) or the "VIBRATION AUTO ADJUSTMENT" icon at the
            SERVONAVI M menu screen, the vibration auto adjustment screen appears.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                      ME32123R0705000160001

                                                  Fig.43-12 Vibration auto adjustment screen
            With vibration auto adjustment, the vibration frequency is specified using predefined dedicated axis
            movements, and the servo control parameters are changed to optimum values based on the
            specified vibration frequency.
            If function key [F8] (END) is pressed, the display returns to the SERVONAVI M menu screen.
                                                                                                                                6103-E P-318
                                                                                        SECTION 43 SERVONAVI M FUNCTION

43-6-1.Vibration Auto Adjustment Operation

            The procedure up to updating the servo control parameters with vibration auto adjustment is as
            follows.

            (1) Set the rapid traverse override and cutting feed override to 100%.

            (2) Press function key [F1] (START) in MDI operation mode.
                   By pressing function key [F1] (START), the command used to specify the vibration frequency
                   set at "G459 CONDITION" is sent to the MDI operation buffer.
                    * A reset is performed before sending the command to the MDI operation buffer, and
                        therefore caution is required.

                    * Refer to "43-6-2. Specifying G459 Operating Conditions" for details on G459 operating
                        conditions.

            (3) By pressing the [START] button, vibration frequency specification is started at the selected axis,
                   and axis travel is performed. When doing so, "RUNNING" lights up adjacent to "CONTROL
                   PARA" for the axis for which vibration auto adjustment is being performed.
                   Details of axis travel are given below.

                   a. The axis moves in the plus direction by travel distance L only and at feed rate F1.

                   b. The axis makes ten 1 m return journeys over approximately 5 seconds.

                   c. The axis moves in the minus direction by travel distance L only and at feed rate F2.

                   d. The axis makes ten 10 m return journeys over approximately 5 seconds.

                   e. The axis moves in the plus direction by travel distance L only while switching between feed
                         rate Fa to Fd.

                   f. The axis makes ten 50 m return journeys over approximately 5 seconds.

                   g. The axis moves in the minus direction by travel distance L only while switching between
                         feed rate Fa to Fd.

                   h. The axis moves in the plus direction by travel distance L only and at feed rate Fb.

                   i. The axis moves in the minus direction by travel distance L only and at feed rate Fa, and
                         then returns to the position prior to vibration auto adjustment.

            (4) The servo control parameters are updated based on the specified vibration frequency.

            (5) After the servo control parameters have been updated, the "RUNNING" lamp adjacent to
                   "CONTROL PARA" for the axis for which vibration auto adjustment was performed turns off and
                   "COMPLETE" lights up.

            Note
            1) If the rapid traverse override and cutting feed override are not set to 100%, an alarm will occur

                 when starting to specify the vibration frequency.
            2) The machining fold condition may differ before and after vibration auto adjustment.

            [Supplement]

              If the specified vibration frequency exceeds the specified value set in the machine beforehand, an
              alarm occurs.
                                                                                                                                6103-E P-319
                                                                                        SECTION 43 SERVONAVI M FUNCTION

43-6-2.Specifying G459 Operating Conditions

            The command used to specify vibration frequency is displayed in "G459 CONDITION".

            frequency can be changed.
            The optimum values for travel distance and feed rate are automatically determined for each axis,
            and therefore cannot be changed directly.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                      ME32123R0705000180001

                               Fig.43-13 Vibration auto adjustment screen: Screen when [F6] pressed
            The axis that travels when specifying vibration frequency can be changed here. The changed axis
            will be the axis that is adjusted.
            If the axis is changed, the travel distance and feed rate are automatically changed based on the
            changed axis.
            When doing so, the axis, travel distance, and feed rate values displayed on the screen are updated
            together with the G459 operating conditions.
            If function key [F1] (CANCEL) is pressed, the axis is not changed, and the display returns to the
            previous screen.
            [Supplement]

              The axes that can be selected are determined beforehand based on the machine model. Axes
              that cannot be selected are grayed out, and the axes are not changed even when pressing the
              applicable function key.

44-2. Outline of Buttons

[Any angle]
Allows axis feed to be performed with the slope coordinate system.
The button lamp lights up while the slope coordinate system mode is ON.
The display coordinate system in the LCD is not changed.

[Distance]
Displays the residual distance. When the [Distance] button is pressed with the residual distance
displayed, the current position is displayed.
When the residual distance is displayed, the button lamp lights up

[Page change]
Changes the axis to be displayed in the LCD.
The axis to be displayed is the one set in Page 1 and Page 2 in the PULSE HANDLE PARAMETER
screen (see 44-3. PULSE HANDLE PARAMETER Screen) in the PARAMETER screen.
Pressing this button alternates the set axis between Page 1 and Page 2.

[Coor. change]
Each time this coordinate system selection button is pressed, the coordinate system is switched as
follows.

Machine  workpiece       slope            3D                           machine  ...
                             coordinate       coordinate                   coordinate
coordinate  coordinate       system           system                       system

system      system

The coordinate system is switched only on the LCD display and not switched with the NC.

* The slope coordinate system and the 3D coordinate system cannot be selected with some
    machine models. The coordinate systems that cannot be selected are not indicated with the
    lamp on the LED.

[3D]
Allows operations with the 3D coordinate system.
When the 3D coordinate system mode is ON, the button lamp lights up.
The coordinate system displayed on the LCD is not changed.

* Button(s) [3D] or/and [Angle] may not be provided with some machine models.

                                                                                                                                6103-E P-334
                                                                        SECTION 45 CYCLE TIME REDUCTION FUNCTION

SECTION 45 CYCLE TIME REDUCTION FUNCTION

45-1. Overview

            The cycle time reduction mode is a function that reduces the cycle time of part cutting in which
            cutting with short feed axis travel distance and short positioning are frequently performed alternately
            compared to die machining.
            The operation is carried out in the conventional manner when the cycle time reduction mode is OFF.
            When the cycle time reduction mode is ON, the operation is carried out in the mode in which cutting
            feeds and rapid traverse feeds are switched to each other faster.
            Even when the cycle time reduction mode is set to ON, the operation is carried out with the cycle
            time reduction mode OFF if "Hi-CUT Pro CONTROL" and/or "Super-NURBS CONTROL" are/is
            OFF.
            When the cycle time reduction mode is ON, the parameters of "Hi-CUT Pro CONTROL" and "Super-
            NURBS CONTROL" are also changed to those for the cycle time reduction mode. Note that the
            parameters of "Hi-CUT Pro CONTROL" and "Super-NURBS CONTROL" set with CYCLE TIME
            REDUCTION MODE OFF are not reflected to the parameters of CYCLE TIME REDUCTION MODE
            ON, and vice versa. (The parameters of "Hi-CUT Pro CONTROL" and "Super-NURBS CONTROL"
            set with CYCLE TIME REDUCTION MODE ON are not reflected to the parameters of CYCLE TIME
            REDUCTION MODE OFF.)

             * Under some cutting conditions, the cycle time may be longer when CYCLE TIME REDUCTION
                 MODE is set to ON than when CYCLE TIME REDUCTION MODE is set to OFF.

             * Cutting is performed with CYCLE TIME REDUCTION MODE OFF during the machine lock ON.
             * When the cycle time reduction mode is ON, cutting may be accompanied by machine vibration

                 depending on the machine installation condition. In that case, reduce the acceleration by setting
                 "ACCELERATION SELECT PARA." to be described later to perform cutting while suppressing
                 machine vibration.

45-2. How to Set Parameters

45-2-1.CYCLE TIME REDUCTION PARAMETER screen

            Procedure :

           1 Select the parameter setting mode.

           2 Press [F8] (DISPLAY CHANGE).
                                                                                                                6103-E P-335
                                                        SECTION 45 CYCLE TIME REDUCTION FUNCTION

3 Press the  cursor keys to locate the highlighted cursor to "NC optional parameter (CYCLE

    TIME REDUCTION PARAMETER)" in the menu, and then press [F8] (CLOSE).

                                                                                                                                                                 ME32123R0705400020001

                             DISPLAY CHANGE pop-up window for parameter setting

4 Set CYCLE TIME REDUCTION MODE and ACCELERATION SELECT PARA. in the screen

    below.

                                                                                                                                                                                     ME32123R0705400020002

                                  CYCLE TIME REDUCTION PARAMETER screen

45-2-2.Details of cycle time reduction mode

            Set CYCLE TIME REDUCTION MODE to ON or OFF.
                                                                     [Function menu]

                                                                                                                                                                                                                     ME32123R0705400030001

            When set to OFF, cutting is performed with CYCLE TIME REDUCTION MODE OFF.
            When set to ON, cutting is performed with CYCLE TIME REDUCTION MODE ON.
            After the mode is changed, the power needs to be cycled once.
            The mode cannot be changed during a program operation.

45-2-3.Details of acceleration select parameter

                                                                                                                                                                                           ME32123R0705400040001

ACCELERATION SELECT PARA. is effective only when CYCLE TIME REDUCTION MODE is set
to ON.

                                                        [Function menu]

                                                                                                                                                                                           ME32123R0705400040002

ACCELERATION SELECT PARA. has been set to HIGH as default. The acceleration value of G00/
G01 changes according to the set value.
If cutting is accompanied by machine vibration due to the machine installation conditions, change
this parameter from HIGH to MID before performing cutting. If the vibration is not still suppressed,
select LOW or CUSTOM, and set an optimum acceleration rate.

ACCELERATION    G00 ACCEL [%]                         G01 ACCEL [%]
SELECT PARA.
                                               100                                   100
        HIGH                                    75                                    75
         MID                                    50                                    50
        LOW
     CUSTOM   NC optional parameter word            NC optional parameter word
                                          No.138                                No.139

* Unit: %

* This parameter cannot be changed when CYCLE TIME REDUCTION MODE is set to OFF.

* This parameter cannot be changed during a program operation (including STM lock, slide
     holed, and single block stop).

* After this parameter is changed, alarm D "4336 Reset effective parameter set" appears. Press
     the NC RESET button to remove it.

CUSTOM.

   Initial value  NC optional parameter word
Maximum value                    No.138
Minimum value                                              25
                                                         200
                                                            1

* Unit: %
* This parameter cannot be changed when CYCLE TIME REDUCTION MODE is set to OFF.
* This parameter cannot be changed during a program operation (including STM lock, slide holed,

   and single block stop).
* Setting a value exceeding 100% may shorten the machine life and tool life.
* If set to 200%, double acceleration rate is applied as the standard machine.
* If set to 10% or less, the rate may be replaced to 10%.
* After this parameter is changed, alarm D "4336 Reset effective parameter set" appears. Press

   the NC RESET button to remove it.

45-2-4.Details of fixed cycle

IN-P check during fixed cycle mode is performed based on optional parameter word No.137 only
when the cycle time reduction mode is ON.
When the cycle time reduction mode is OFF, IN-P check is performed based on the in-position value
set in the system parameter.

                               m [0.001mm]                        0.1m [0.0001mm]

   Initial value                                 0                                       0
Maximum value                               1000                                   10000
Minimum value
                                                 0                                       0

When set to 0, the in-position value set in the system parameter is used.
When the cycle time reduction mode is OFF, optional parameter word No.137 cannot be changed.

 List of corresponding fixed cycles

   This function is effective during the following fixed cycles.

[Fixed cycle table]  Description
 G code              High-speed deep hole drilling cycle
 G73                 Reverse tapping cycle (float tapping)
 G74                 Fine boring
 G76                 Drilling cycle
 G81,82              Deep hole drilling cycle
 G83                 Tapping cycle (float tapping)
 G84                 Boring cycle
 G85,89              Boring cycle
 G86                 Back boring cycle
 G87                 Deep hole reverse tapping cycle
 G272                High-speed deep hole reverse tapping cycle
 G273                Reverse tapping cycle
 G274                Deep hole drilling fixed cycle 2
 G281                Deep hole tapping cycle
 G282                High-speed deep hole tapping cycle
 G283                Tapping cycle
 G284

                                                                  * Excluding synchronized tapping

45-3. Parameters Related to Cycle Time Reduction

            The parameters related to the cycle time reduction function are described.
            The cycle time may be lengthened depending on the cutting detail.

45-3-1.Optional parameter bit

No.  bit          Bit                Description

67   1                 The M3 command in the high-speed deep hole drilling (G73, G81,
                       G82, G83, G85, G89) fixed cycle is ignored.

                       The M3 command in the high-speed deep hole drilling (G73, G81,
                       G82, G83, G85, G89) fixed cycle is not ignored.

67   2                 The R- point command in the M54 command (R-point level return)
                       and in a fixed cycle excluding a back boring cycle (G87) is
                       ignored.

                       The R- point command in the M54 command (R-point level return)
                       and in a fixed cycle excluding a back boring cycle (G87) is not
                       ignored.

              4        The spindle stop command (M5) at the hole bottom Z point in float
67                     tapping (G74, G84) is effective.

                       The spindle stop command (M5) at the hole bottom Z point in float
                       tapping (G74, G84) is not effective.

               3       Super-NURBS control/Hi-CUT Pro control is effective in a fixed
109                    cycle (G73/G81/G83).

                       Super-NURBS control/Hi-CUT Pro control is not effective in a
                       fixed cycle (G73/G81/G83).

45-3-2.Optional parameter long word

No.                    Description

1 Escape amount of G73 cycle (high-speed deep hole) (0.0001 mm)

2 Escape amount of G83 cycle (deep hole) (0.0001 mm)
